function u95.GetSubjectCFrame(a1) -- Line: 202
    local lastSubjectCFrame = a1.lastSubjectCFrame
    local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local CameraSubject = CurrentCamera and CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

    if not CameraSubject then
        return lastSubjectCFrame
    end

    if CameraSubject:IsA("Humanoid") then
        local v1 = (CameraSubject:GetState()) == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
        local CameraOffset = CameraSubject.CameraOffset

        if a1:GetIsMouseLocked() then
            CameraOffset = Vector3.new()
        end

        local RootPart = CameraSubject.RootPart

        if v1 and CameraSubject.Parent and CameraSubject.Parent:IsA("Model") then
            RootPart = CameraSubject.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head") or RootPart
        end

        if RootPart and RootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
            local v2

            if CameraSubject.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
            elseif not CameraSubject.AutomaticScalingEnabled then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            else
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                local RootPart_2 = CameraSubject.RootPart

                if RootPart == RootPart_2 then
                    v2 = v2 + Vector3.new(0, (RootPart_2.Size.Y - 2) / 2, 0)
                end
            end

            if v1 then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end

            lastSubjectCFrame = RootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(v2 + CameraOffset)
        end
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("BasePart") then
        lastSubjectCFrame = CameraSubject.CFrame
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("Model") then
        lastSubjectCFrame = if not CameraSubject.PrimaryPart then
            CFrame.new()
        else
            CameraSubject:GetPrimaryPartCFrame()
    end

    if lastSubjectCFrame then
        a1.lastSubjectCFrame = lastSubjectCFrame
    end

    return lastSubjectCFrame
end

    local CameraSubject = CurrentCamera and CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

    if not CameraSubject then
        return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    if CameraSubject:IsA("BasePart") then
        return CameraSubject.Velocity
    end

    if CameraSubject:IsA("Humanoid") then
        local RootPart = CameraSubject.RootPart

        if RootPart then
            return RootPart.Velocity
        end

        return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    if CameraSubject:IsA("Model") then
        local PrimaryPart = CameraSubject.PrimaryPart

        if PrimaryPart then
            return PrimaryPart.Velocity
        end
    end

    return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
end

function u95.StepZoom(a1, a2) -- Line: 327; upvalues: CameraInput (val), u52 (val); types: a1: table, a2: number?
    local currentSubjectDistance = a1.currentSubjectDistance
    local v1 = CameraInput.getZoomDelta(a2)

function u95:GetSubjectPosition() -- Line: 352
    local lastSubjectPosition = self.lastSubjectPosition
    local CurrentCamera = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local CameraSubject = CurrentCamera and CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

    if not CameraSubject then
        return nil
    end

    if CameraSubject:IsA("Humanoid") then
        local v1 = (CameraSubject:GetState()) == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
        local CameraOffset = CameraSubject.CameraOffset

        if self:GetIsMouseLocked() then
            CameraOffset = Vector3.new()
        end

        local RootPart = CameraSubject.RootPart

        if v1 and CameraSubject.Parent and CameraSubject.Parent:IsA("Model") then
            RootPart = CameraSubject.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head") or RootPart
        end

        if RootPart and RootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
            local v2

            if CameraSubject.RigType ~= Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
            elseif not CameraSubject.AutomaticScalingEnabled then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            else
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)

                if RootPart == CameraSubject.RootPart then
                    v2 = v2 + Vector3.new(0, CameraSubject.RootPart.Size.Y / 2 - 1, 0)
                end
            end

            if v1 then
                v2 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end

            lastSubjectPosition = RootPart.CFrame.Position
                + RootPart.CFrame:vectorToWorldSpace(v2 + CameraOffset)
        end
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
        lastSubjectPosition = CameraSubject.CFrame.Position
            + CameraSubject.CFrame:vectorToWorldSpace((Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)))
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("SkateboardPlatform") then
        lastSubjectPosition = CameraSubject.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("BasePart") then
        lastSubjectPosition = CameraSubject.CFrame.Position
    elseif CameraSubject:IsA("Model") then
        lastSubjectPosition = if not CameraSubject.PrimaryPart then
            CameraSubject:GetModelCFrame().Position
        else
            CameraSubject:GetPrimaryPartCFrame().Position
    end

    self.lastSubject = CameraSubject
    self.lastSubjectPosition = lastSubjectPosition

    return lastSubjectPosition
end

function u95:GamepadZoomPress() -- Line: 457; upvalues: LocalPlayer (val)
    local CameraMinZoomDistance
    local CameraToSubjectDistance = self:GetCameraToSubjectDistance()
    local CameraMaxZoomDistance = LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance

    for i = #self.gamepadZoomLevels, 1, -1 do
        CameraMinZoomDistance = self.gamepadZoomLevels[i]

        if not (CameraMaxZoomDistance < CameraMinZoomDistance) then
            if CameraMinZoomDistance < LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance then
                CameraMinZoomDistance = LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance

                if CameraMaxZoomDistance == CameraMinZoomDistance then
                    break
                end
            end

            if CameraMinZoomDistance + (CameraMaxZoomDistance - CameraMinZoomDistance) / 2
                < CameraToSubjectDistance then
                self:SetCameraToSubjectDistance(CameraMinZoomDistance)
                return
            end

            CameraMaxZoomDistance = CameraMinZoomDistance
        end
    end

    self:SetCameraToSubjectDistance(self.gamepadZoomLevels[#self.gamepadZoomLevels])
end

function u95:SetCameraToSubjectDistance(a2)
    -- Line: 591
    -- upvalues: LocalPlayer (val), u52 (val)
    -- types: self: table, a2: number
    local currentSubjectDistance = self.currentSubjectDistance

    if LocalPlayer.CameraMode ~= Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson then
        local v1 = math.clamp(a2, LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance, LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance)

        if not (v1 < 1) then
            self.currentSubjectDistance = v1

            if self.inFirstPerson then

            end
        end

        self.currentSubjectDistance = 0.5

            self:EnterFirstPerson()
        end
    end

    u52.SetZoomParameters(self.currentSubjectDistance, (math.sign(a2 - currentSubjectDistance)))

    return self.currentSubjectDistance
end

function u16.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(a1) -- Line: 192

        return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow
    end

    if a1 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Default then
        return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
    end

    if a1 ~= Enum.TouchCameraMovementMode.Classic
        and a1 ~= Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.Classic
        and a1 ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
        and a1 ~= Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic then
        if a1 ~= Enum.TouchCameraMovementMode.Follow
            and a1 ~= Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.Follow
            and a1 ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow
            and a1 ~= Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow then

                and a1 ~= Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.Orbital
                and a1 ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital
                and a1 ~= Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital then
                if a1 ~= Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.CameraToggle
                    and a1 ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.CameraToggle then
                    if a1 ~= Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.UserChoice
                        and a1 ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
                        return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
                    end

                    return Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice
                end

            end

            return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital
        end

        return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow
    end

    return Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
end

local function Det3x3(a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9)
    -- Line: 87
    -- types: a1: number, a2: number, a3: number, a4: number, a5: number, a6: number, a7: number, a8: number, a9: number
    return a1 * (a5 * a9 - a6 * a8) - a2 * (a4 * a9 - a6 * a7)
        + a3 * (a4 * a8 - a5 * a7)
end

local function RayIntersection(a1, a2, a3, a4) -- Line: 105; types: a1: vector, a2: vector, a3: vector, a4: vector
    local v1 = a2:Cross(a4)
    local v2 = a3.X - a1.X
    local v3 = a3.Y - a1.Y
    local v4 = a3.Z - a1.Z
    local X = a2.X
    local v5 = -a4.X
    local X_2 = v1.X
    local Y = a2.Y
    local v6 = -a4.Y
    local Y_2 = v1.Y
    local Z = a2.Z
    local v7 = -a4.Z
    local Z_2 = v1.Z
    local v8 = X * (v6 * Z_2 - Y_2 * v7) - v5 * (Y * Z_2 - Y_2 * Z)
        + X_2 * (Y * v7 - v6 * Z)

    if v8 == 0 then
        return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end

    local v9 = -a4.X
    local X_3 = v1.X
    v6 = -a4.Y
    local Y_3 = v1.Y
    local v10 = -a4.Z
    local Z_3 = v1.Z
    local v11 = (v2 * (v6 * Z_3 - Y_3 * v10) - v9 * (v3 * Z_3 - Y_3 * v4)
        + X_3 * (v3 * v10 - v6 * v4))
        / v8
    local X_4 = a2.X
    local X_5 = v1.X
    local Y_4 = a2.Y
    local Y_5 = v1.Y
    local Z_4 = a2.Z
    local Z_5 = v1.Z
    v5 = (X_4 * (v3 * Z_5 - Y_5 * v4) - v2 * (Y_4 * Z_5 - Y_5 * Z_4)
        + X_5 * (Y_4 * v4 - v3 * Z_4))
        / v8
    v8 = a1 + v11 * a2
    v11 = a3 + v5 * a4
    v5 = v8 + (v11 - v8) * 0.5

    if (v11 - v8).Magnitude < 0.25 then
        return v5
    end

    return (Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
end

function u68:updateImmersionAutoRotate() -- Line: 491; upvalues: VRService (val)

local function testPromotion(a1, a2, a3)

function u0:restoreSimulatedRotation() -- Line: 250
    local renderedRotationOverride = self.renderedRotationOverride

    if renderedRotationOverride == nil then
        return
    end

    if renderedRotationOverride.rootPart.Parent ~= nil then
        renderedRotationOverride.rootPart.CFrame = (CFrame.new(renderedRotationOverride.rootPart.CFrame.Position))
            * renderedRotationOverride.simulatedRotation
    end

    self.renderedRotationOverride = nil
end

function u0:applyRenderedRotation() -- Line: 261; upvalues: Workspace (val)
    local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local humanoid = self.humanoid
    local RootPart = humanoid and humanoid.RootPart

    if CurrentCamera ~= nil
        and RootPart ~= nil
        and not humanoid.Sit
        and humanoid.SeatPart == nil
        and not RootPart:IsGrounded()
        and (humanoid:GetState()) ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
        local LookVector = CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
        local v1 = Vector3.new(LookVector.X, 0, LookVector.Z)

        if v1.Magnitude < 0.001 then
            return
        end

        if self.renderedRotationOverride ~= nil
            and self.renderedRotationOverride.rootPart ~= RootPart then
            self:restoreSimulatedRotation()
        end

        if self.renderedRotationOverride == nil then
            self.renderedRotationOverride = {rootPart = RootPart, simulatedRotation = RootPart.CFrame.Rotation}
        end

        local Position = RootPart.CFrame.Position
        RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Position, Position + v1)
    end
end

        if v3 > 3.141592653589793 then

            self.touchJumpController:Enable(false)

function u0:calculateRawMoveVector(a2, a3)
    -- Line: 718
    -- upvalues: Workspace (val), VRService (val), u245 (val)
    -- types: self: table, a2: userdata, a3: vector
    local v1, v2
    local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not CurrentCamera then
        return a3
    end

    local CFrame_2 = CurrentCamera.CFrame

    if VRService.VREnabled and a2.RootPart then
        local EstimatedVRTorsoFrame = self:GetEstimatedVRTorsoFrame()
        CFrame_2 = if not ((CurrentCamera.Focus.Position - CFrame_2.Position).Magnitude
            < 3) then
            CurrentCamera.CFrame
                * (EstimatedVRTorsoFrame.Rotation
                    + EstimatedVRTorsoFrame.Position * CurrentCamera.HeadScale)
        else
            CFrame_2 * EstimatedVRTorsoFrame
    end

    if (a2:GetState()) ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
        local Components_10, Components_11, Components_12, Components_3, Components_9, v3
        _, _, _, Components_9, Components_10, Components_11, _, _, Components_3, _, _, Components_12 = CFrame_2:GetComponents()

        if not (Components_3 < 1) or not (Components_3 > -1) then
            v1 = Components_9
            v3 = -Components_10 * math.sign(Components_3)
        else
            v1 = Components_12
            v3 = Components_11
        end

        local v4 = math.sqrt(v1 * v1 + v3 * v3)

        v2 = v2.Rotation + v2.Position * CurrentCamera.HeadScale
        v1 = -0.7 * self.humanoid.RootPart.Size.Y / 2

function u0.ProcessInputs(a1, a2, a3)
    -- Line: 1160
    -- upvalues: UserPlayerScriptsCCLIntegrationD (val), u217 (ref), UserPlayerScriptsPlayerControlState2 (val)
    -- upvalues: InputReplication (val)

            return
        end

            return
        end

        InputReplication.SendInputToHumanoidForServerAuth(a2)

        return
    end

    if UserPlayerScriptsPlayerControlState2 then
        InputReplication.processPCSInputs(a2)
        return
    end

    local Character = a2.Character

    if Character == nil then
        return
    end

    local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if Humanoid == nil then
        return
    end

    local v1 = a2:FindFirstChild("InputContexts")

    if v1 == nil then
        return
    end

    local CharacterContext = v1:FindFirstChild("CharacterContext")

    if CharacterContext == nil then
        return
    end

    local MoveAction = CharacterContext.MoveAction
    local RotationAction = CharacterContext.RotationAction
    local JumpAction = CharacterContext.JumpAction

    local function isValidInput2D(a1) -- Line: 1196; types: a1: userdata
        local v1 = true

        if a1.X == a1.X then
            v1 = true

            if a1.Y == a1.Y then
                v1 = true

                if a1.X ~= (1 / 0) then
                    v1 = a1.Y == (1 / 0)
                end
            end
        end

        return not v1
    end

    local function isValidInput3D(a1) -- Line: 1205; types: a1: vector
        local v1 = true

        if a1.X == a1.X then
            v1 = true

            if a1.Y == a1.Y then
                v1 = true

                if a1.Z == a1.Z then
                    v1 = true

                    if a1.X ~= (1 / 0) then
                        v1 = true

                        if a1.Y ~= (1 / 0) then
                            v1 = a1.Z == (1 / 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return not v1
    end

    local State = if MoveAction == nil then Vector2.new(0, 0) else MoveAction:GetState()
    local LookVector = a2:GetCameraState().CFrame.LookVector
    local v2 = true

    if State.X == State.X then
        v2 = true

        if State.Y == State.Y then
            v2 = true

            if State.X ~= (1 / 0) then
                v2 = State.Y == (1 / 0)
            end
        end
    end

    if not v2 then
        local v3 = true

        if LookVector.X == LookVector.X then
            v3 = true

            if LookVector.Y == LookVector.Y then
                v3 = true

                if LookVector.Z == LookVector.Z then
                    v3 = true

                    if LookVector.X ~= (1 / 0) then
                        v3 = true

                        if LookVector.Y ~= (1 / 0) then
                            v3 = LookVector.Z == (1 / 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not v3 and 0 < LookVector.Magnitude then
            if (Humanoid:GetState()) ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
                LookVector = Vector3.new(LookVector.X, 0, LookVector.Z).Unit
            end

            local Unit = (LookVector:Cross((Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)))).Unit
            Humanoid:Move(LookVector * State.Y + Unit * State.X)

            if not RotationAction:GetState() then
                Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            else
                Humanoid.AutoRotate = false

                if Humanoid.SeatPart == nil
                    and Humanoid.RootPart ~= nil
                    and not Humanoid.Sit
                    and not Humanoid.RootPart:IsGrounded() then
                    Humanoid.RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(Humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.Position, Humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.Position + LookVector)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    Humanoid.Jump = if JumpAction == nil then false else JumpAction:GetState()
end

function u53.processPCSInputs(a1)
    -- Line: 271
    -- upvalues: UserAbilitiesUserInterfaceB (val), u52 (val)
    -- types: a1: userdata

    end

    if Humanoid == nil then
        return
    end

        Character:FindFirstChild(u52)
    else
        Character:FindFirstChildOfClass(u52))
        == nil then
        return
    end

    local Move = v2.Move
    local Jump = v2.Jump
    local RotateToLookDirection = v2.RotateToLookDirection
    local LookDirection = v2.LookDirection

    end

    Humanoid.Jump = Jump or false

    -- upvalues: u140 (val), u153 (val), Config (val), u199 (ref), StackKey (val), u209 (ref), u200 (ref), u207 (ref)
    -- upvalues: u201 (ref), u208 (val)
    local v1
    local v2 = {}
    local v3 = nil
    local v4 = nil

    for i, j in u140, v3, v4 do
        for k, n2 in u153.equipmentFor(j) do
            v2[n2] = true
        end
    end

    for m in Config.Wearable.Items do
        v2[m] = true
    end

    local v5 = {}

    for i5, i6 in u199 do
        if v2[StackKey.item(i5)] then
            table.insert(v5, (("c%*=%*"):format(i5, i6)))
        end
    end

    for i7, i8 in u209 do
        if v2[i8.itemId] then
            v1 = StackKey.roll(i8.outcome, i8.perfect, i8.stats)
            table.insert(v5, (("g%*=%*"):format(i8.n, (StackKey.of(i8.itemId, v1)))))
        end
    end

    for i9, i10 in u200 do
        table.insert(v5, (("e%*=%*"):format(i9, i10)))
    end

    for i11, i12 in u207 do
        table.insert(v5, (("n%*=%*"):format(i11, i12)))
    end

    for i13, i14 in u201 do
        table.insert(v5, (("w%*=%*"):format(i13, i14)))
    end

    for i15, i16 in u208 do
        table.insert(v5, (("x%*=%*"):format(i15, i16)))
    end

    table.sort(v5)

    return table.concat(v5, ";")
end
